# MATLAB to Python: functions and parameters

Generated by `python tools/gen_mapping.py` from the parameter registry
(`petakit5d.params`); do not edit it by hand. CI fails when it is out of date.

- **registered**: the port exists; its spec gives the Python function, the MATLAB-name
  function `petakit5d.matlab.<MATLAB name>` and the command line (`pk5d-master <MATLAB name>`).
- **planned**: the name the port will have (PLAN.md section 3.3), for review.
- Parameters keep MATLAB's names in snake_case (`xyPixelSize` -> `xy_pixel_size`,
  `DSRDirName` -> `dsr_dir_name`, `save16bit` -> `save_16bit`); the MATLAB layer takes the
  MATLAB names in any case and unique prefixes, as inputParser does.
- Third-party MATLAB code (a file whose header names an outside author): the table shows its
  interface (names, kinds, order and defaults) and `(third-party: not shown)` in place of
  MATLAB's validators, whose texts the repository does not keep, with the port's own check
  where a registered function has one (`accepts=`, made from MATLAB's behaviour).

127 registered, 2 planned, 26 not ported.

## Parameters

Arg: req (required, positional), opt (optional, positional), par (name-value; keyword-only in Python), pos (positional; MATLAB fills defaults by nargin). Conversion: the parameter's kind (params/kinds.py: the MATLAB-name layer, the command line and parameter files convert MATLAB's value to the Python one and back; empty: none; '<task> decides': open). The MATLAB default in MATLAB syntax, the Python default as the snake_case function takes it. Registered functions show their spec; planned ones MATLAB's inputParser or arguments block under the naming rule and the planned kinds (params/planned.py).

### `cleanXmlFile` -> `petakit5d.io.n5.clean_xml_file`

Remove the white space around the texts of ``<BasePath type="relative">`` and ``<n5 type="relative">`` (``cleanXmlFile``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fileName` | `file_name` | req |  |  |  |  | XML file, changed in place. |

### `combinePhases` -> `petakit5d.sim.file.combine_phases_file`

Interleave ``<name>_phase<p>.tif`` again into ``<name>_combined.tif`` (single; ``combinePhases``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataFile` | `data_file` | req |  |  |  |  | The name the phase files derive from (<name>_phase<p>.tif). |
| `nphases` | `nphases` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Phases. |

### `compute_tile_bwdist` -> `petakit5d.stitch.distance.compute_tile_bwdist`

``compute_tile_bwdist``: the distance map of tile ``tile_ind`` of the block information (``tile_distance_map`` with its overlap regions), written as a native single zarr (chunks ``block_size``, zstd; the separator ``/`` above 10,000 chunks) under a temporary name and renamed to ``bwdist_fullpath``. Nothing is done if that exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Block information file (stitching). |
| `tileInd` | `tile_ind` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isscalar` | The tile (0-based) in the block information. |
| `bwdistFullpath` | `bwdist_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Distance map to write (zarr). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Power of the weights (an integer). |
| `singleDistMap` | `single_dist_map` | req |  |  |  | `@islogical` | The map from the tile alone, not its overlap regions. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the map. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Not used (as in MATLAB: zstd). |
| `distBbox` | `dist_bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of full weight (None: none). |
| `dataOrderMat` | `data_order_axes` | req | custom:data_order_axes_from_matlab |  |  | `@(x) isvector(x)` | Array axes of x, y and z of the tile (stitch.geometry.parse_data_order). (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | opt |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace a map that appears while this one is computed. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the map and the zarr reads and writes (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs` -> `petakit5d.stitch.distance.compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs`

``compute_tile_bwdist_mip_slabs``: the map of tile ``tile_ind``'s z MIP slab (``<tile folder>/MIP_Slabs_pooling_<pool>/<name>_z.zarr``; ``tile_distance_map_mip_slab``), written as a native single zarr with ``shard_size`` and ``compressor`` under a temporary name and renamed to ``bwdist_fullpath``. Nothing is done if that exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Block information file (stitching). |
| `tileInd` | `tile_ind` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isscalar` | The tile (0-based) in the block information. |
| `bwdistFullpath` | `bwdist_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Distance map to write (zarr). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Power of the weights, applied in the blending. |
| `singleDistMap` | `single_dist_map` | req |  |  |  | `@islogical` | Not used (always the tile's own map). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the map. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Shard size (z, y, x) (None: none). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Compressor of the map. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isvector` | Pool sizes (z, y, x) of the MIP slabs. |

| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | opt |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace a map that appears while this one is computed. |

### `constructXmlFile` -> `petakit5d.io.n5.construct_xml_file`

The BigStitcher XML of the tiles of an image list (``constructXmlFile``): returns the struct ``s`` (nested dicts and lists in MATLAB's field order; None is MATLAB's ``missing``) for ``write_struct_xml``, the tiles' table and the voxel size along the N5 dimensions. Tiles are sorted by camera, channel, z, y, x, t and iteration and kept if they match a channel pattern and exist in ``data_path``; their stage coordinates go through ``axis_order`` and start at 0, and ``data_order`` (``'zyx'`` for standard N5, as ``zarr_to_n5`` writes it; ``'yxz'`` for MATLAB's zarrToN5) maps them, the voxel size and the tile sizes to the N5 dimensions; ``xcorr_info_fn`` adds the stitching shifts.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPath` | `data_path` | req |  |  |  |  | Folder of the tiles (ends with a separator). |
| `fileName` | `file_name` | req |  |  |  |  | The N5 container the XML names (its name and extension are used). |
| `csvFile` | `csv_file` | req |  |  |  |  | Image list (csv) of the tiles. |
| `voxelSize` | `voxel_size` | req | per_axis_xyz |  |  |  | Voxel size (z, y, x). |
| `ChannelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | opt |  | `{'CamA'}` | `('CamA',)` |  | Channel identifiers of the tiles to include. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | opt |  | `false` | `False` |  | The tiles are zarr (else tiff). |
| `axisOrder` | `axis_order` | opt | same | `'xyz'` | `'xyz'` |  | Axis order of the stage coordinates ('xyz', '-x,y,z', ...). |
| `dataOrder` | `data_order` | opt | same | `'zyx'` | `'zyx'` |  | Axes of the N5 datasets' C-order arrays: 'zyx' (standard N5, zarr_to_n5's), 'yxz' (MATLAB's zarrToN5). (default differs from MATLAB: the port writes standard N5 (dimensions [nx, ny, nz]), MATLAB [nz, nx, ny]) |
| `xcorrInfoFn` | `xcorr_info_fn` | opt |  | `''` | `''` |  | Stitching xcorr result (.mat with dxyz_shift; '': none). |

### `createzarr` -> `petakit5d.io.zarr.create_zarr`

Create an empty native zarr array (MATLAB ``createzarr``): an existing folder of that name is removed, then the ``.zarray`` is written (to a temporary name, then renamed).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` | Zarr array to create (an existing folder is replaced). |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `(1, 1) logical` | Accepted; the folder is always replaced, as in MATLAB. |
| `dataSize` | `data_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Size (z, y, x), or (y, x) of an image. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Inner chunk size (z, y, x) of a sharded Zarr v3 array (empty: no sharding). |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par | class_name | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `char` | Class: 'uint8', 'uint16', 'single' or 'double' (or its NumPy type). |
| `order` | `order` | par | same | `'C'` | `'C'` | `char` | MATLAB's order flag; the array is native (z, y, x) in C order either way. (default differs from MATLAB: the port writes native (z, y, x) arrays in C order (decision 0004); MATLAB's 'F' is taken and gives the same array) |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Store an image (y, x) as (1, y, x). |
| `groupWrite` | `group_write` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Make the folder group-writable (not on Windows). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | par |  | `'zstd'` | `'zstd'` | `char` | Blosc compressor ('zstd', 'lz4', ...), 'gzip' or 'none'. |
| `clevel` | `clevel` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `(1, 1) {mustBeNumeric}` | Compression level. |
| `zarrSubSize` | `zarr_sub_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Subfolders (z, y, x) of the chunk files (empty: none). |
| `dimSeparator` | `dim_separator` | par |  | `'.'` | `'.'` | `char` | Chunk key separator: '.' or '/'. |

### `cross_correlation_registration_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.stitch.registration.cross_correlation_registration`

The registration of a leading tile with the tiles of its pairs, saved to ``xcorr_fullpath`` (``relative_shift_mat``, ``max_xcorr_mat``, 1-based ``pair_indices``; with ``large_file`` also each pair's slab results): 2D pairs with ``stitch_2d``, large tiles on their MIP slabs (``<xcorr>_mip_slabs.mat`` for one pair, ``<xcorr>_<second tile>_mip_slabs.mat`` for more), else 3D pairs. An existing result is kept.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `imgFullpath_1` | `img_fullpath_1` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | The leading tile (tiff or zarr). |
| `imgFullpath_2` | `img_fullpath_2` | req |  |  |  | `@iscell` | The tiles paired with it. |
| `xcorrFullpath` | `xcorr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Result file (.mat). |
| `pair_indices` | `pair_indices` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | The pairs (k x 2, 0-based tile numbers). |
| `cuboid_1` | `cuboid_1` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Cuboid [x0 y0 z0 x1 y1 z1] (um) of the leading tile. |
| `cuboid_2` | `cuboid_2` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Cuboids (k x 6) of the paired tiles. |
| `cuboid_overlap_12` | `cuboid_overlap_12` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Overlap cuboids (k x 6) of the pairs. |
| `xyz_voxelsizes` | `xyz_voxelsizes` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Voxel sizes (x, y, z, um). |
| `data_order_mat` | `data_order_axes` | req | custom:data_order_axes_from_matlab |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Array axes of x, y and z of the tiles (stitch.geometry.parse_data_order). (renamed) |
| `Stitch2D` | `stitch_2d` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | 2D tiles (normxcorr2). |
| `downSample` | `down_sample` | par | size_yxz | `[1,1,1]` | `(1, 1, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Max pooling (z, y, x) of the regions. |
| `MaxOffset` | `max_offset` | par | per_axis_xyz | `[300,300,50]` | `(50, 300, 300)` | `@isnumeric` | Max offsets (z, y, x, voxels). |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Register the tiles' MIP slabs. |
| `mipDirStr` | `mip_dir_str` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Folder of the MIP slabs next to the tiles. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pool sizes (z, y, x) of the MIP slabs. |
| `dimNumThrsh` | `dim_num_thrsh` | par |  | `10000` | `10000` | `@isnumeric` | Longest template along an axis (cropped by intensity). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the slab tasks on the cluster. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Compiled tasks. |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of each pair's reads and NCC (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `decon_lucy_function` -> `petakit5d.decon.rl.decon_lucy`

Richardson-Lucy deconvolution with acceleration (decon_lucy_function): the result, err_mat, the number of iterations run and the output of ``overlap_fcn`` (see the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `I` | `i` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Data, a (z, y, x) volume or a (y, x) image. |
| `PSF` | `psf` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | PSF (z, y, x), at most the data's size. |
| `NUMIT` | `numit` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | custom:_background_in | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background subtracted from the data: a scalar ([]: 0), or one per voxel. |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | True: on a GPU, an error (decon:noUsableGPU) if none is usable; False: on the CPU; None: a GPU if one is usable, else the CPU with a warning. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Return uint16 (else single). |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor (> 1: results at most dampFactor x data). |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result. |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset added to the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the result to return (None: all). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Error metric and estimates of the iterations. |
| `debug_folder` | `debug_folder` | par |  | `'./debug/'` | `'./debug/'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the debug estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every saveStep iterations (debug). |
| `useMex` | `use_mex` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Iterations in the half-spectrum kernels: None where they can, True required, False MATLAB's loop on full spectra. |
| `overlapFcn` | `overlap_fcn` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x, 'function_handle')` | Function run after the iterations (on the GPU while they run); its output is returned. |

### `deskewData` -> `petakit5d.detection.wrapper.deskew_data`

MATLAB's ``deskewData(data, reverse, 'Overwrite', overwrite, 'SkewAngle', skew_angle, 'aname', aname, ...)`` (module docstring): the DS frames of every movie, ``settings.mat`` and ``dynRange.mat``; returns ``data`` unchanged. Registered with MATLAB's interface and the port's own checks; it takes ``reverse`` (one direction for all movies), ``overwrite``, ``skew_angle``, ``s_cmos_camera_flip`` (MATLAB's error), ``aname`` and ``num_threads``, every other option at its default only (``NotImplementedError`` otherwise). ``aname`` None (MATLAB's ``[]``, the default) is the source folder itself; MATLAB's own default, the handle ``@isstr``, makes every movie fail (W5). ``matlab_quirks`` gives MATLAB's failures: with ``aname`` None, MATLAB's default (``WrapperError``, ``MATLAB:UnableToConvert``; ``''`` is then the source folder), and without movies (``MATLAB:Java:InvalidInput``, W6), where the port returns ``data``.

Third-party MATLAB code: its header names an outside author. Its validators are not shown.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | `data` | opt | custom:_movies_from_matlab | `[]` | `None` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `struct` | The condition (load_condition_data's movies). |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | opt |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Deskew every movie as a reversed scan. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Deskew the movies whose DS frames exist again. |
| `MovieSelector` | `movie_selector` | par |  | `'cell'` | `'cell'` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: the default only. |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only (MATLAB asks for a region). |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `31.5` | `31.5` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `scalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `Rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only (the DSR frames). |
| `CheckFrameMismatch` | `check_frame_mismatch` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |
| `LoadSettings` | `load_settings` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |
| `sCMOSCameraFlip` | `s_cmos_camera_flip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Flip the frames (MATLAB calls a missing function: the same error). |
| `LLFFCorrection` | `llff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |
| `LowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | par |  | `0.4` | `0.4` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `numeric` | Not ported: the default only. |
| `LSImageCh1` | `ls_image_ch_1` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `LSImageCh2` | `ls_image_ch_2` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `LSImageCh3` | `ls_image_ch_3` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `LSImageCh4` | `ls_image_ch_4` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `BackgroundCh1` | `background_ch_1` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `BackgroundCh2` | `background_ch_2` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `BackgroundCh3` | `background_ch_3` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `BackgroundCh4` | `background_ch_4` | par | same | `''` | `''` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Not ported: '' only. |
| `Save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |
| `aname` | `aname` | par |  | `@isstr` | `None` |  | Folder of settings.mat and dynRange.mat in each movie's source folder (None, MATLAB's []: the source folder; with matlab_quirks an aname left out fails, as MATLAB's default does). (default differs from MATLAB: MATLAB's default, the handle @isstr, fails at the first movie (detection.md W5)) |
| `ZoffsetCorrection` | `zoffset_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |
| `matlab_quirks` | `matlab_quirks` | par |  | `false` | `False` |  | MATLAB's failures instead of the port's results: with aname left out (W5) and without movies (W6). (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

| `xStepThresh` | `x_step_thresh` | par |  | `2` | `2.0` | `@isnumeric` | x step above which to interpolate in skewed space. |

### `erodeVolumeBy2DProjection` -> `petakit5d.stitch.tile_functions.erode_volume_by_2d_projection`

Erode the edges of a volume (z, y, x) by ``esize`` voxels through its max projection along y (erodeVolumeBy2DProjection): the voxels whose (z, x) column is eroded in that projection (a square of ``2 esize + 1``, outside the projection as empty) and the first and last ``esize`` rows along y become 0. An image (y, x) keeps its values (MATLAB's ``vol .* (vol ~= 0)``). An ``esize`` of 0 returns ``vol`` itself. ``out``: a C-contiguous array of the input's shape and class to write into, ``vol`` itself included (the projection is taken first).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vol` | `vol` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Volume (z, y, x), or an image (y, x). |
| `esize` | `esize` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Erosion size (voxels). |
| `out` | `out` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Array to write the result into (may be vol itself). (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available); never changes the result. (Python only) |

### `generic_computing_frameworks_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.jobs.framework.run_tasks`

Runs tasks until their outputs exist; returns one done flag per task.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFullpaths` | `input_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Per task a path, or a list of paths; a task whose input is missing is not run. (renamed) |
| `outputFullpaths` | `output_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Per task the path whose existence means the task is done. (renamed) |
| `functionStrs` | `tasks` | req | task_list |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x) \|\| ischar(x)` | Per task a Task (a record dict, a callable without arguments), or with language='bash' a command string; one Task is a list of one. (renamed) |
| `finalOutFullpath` | `final_out_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x)` | stop (all tasks done) once this path exists |
| `clusterType` | `cluster_type` | par |  | `'slurm'` | `'slurm'` | `@ischar` | 'slurm' (cluster jobs or local) or 'parfor' (local process pool) |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB only (compiled jobs); ignored |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | run the tasks as Slurm jobs (if there is a cluster); else in this process |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | this process runs tasks too (in cluster mode: the last waiting batch) |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | folder of the job logs (empty: the default) |
| `tmpDir` | `tmp_dir` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | folder of the .tmp markers of local runs and the call folders (empty: the output folder / the job log folder) |
| `maxCPUNum` | `max_cpu_num` | par |  | `24` | `24` | `@isnumeric` | most CPUs of a job |
| `minCPUNum` | `min_cpu_num` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | fewest CPUs of a job |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job |
| `memPerCPU` | `mem_per_cpu` | par |  | `20.9` | `20.9` | `@isnumeric` | GB of memory per CPU of the jobs |
| `memAllocate` | `mem_allocate` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | GB of memory a task needs (None: unknown); sets the CPUs of its job |
| `wholeNodeJob` | `whole_node_job` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | jobs take whole nodes and run several tasks at once (in-job slots) |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | id of this call (empty: a new one) |
| `GPUJob` | `gpu_job` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | the tasks use a GPU |
| `GPUNum` | `gpu_num` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | GPUs of a whole-node job |
| `maxTrialNum` | `max_trial_num` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | tries of a task |
| `unitWaitTime` | `unit_wait_time` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | minutes a local run leaves a task to another caller whose .tmp marker is newer |
| `maxJobNum` | `max_job_num` | par |  | `Inf` | `inf` | `@isnumeric` | most jobs pending or running |
| `minTaskJobNum` | `min_task_job_num` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | split the tasks into at least this many jobs (whole-node jobs) |
| `taskBatchNum` | `task_batch_num` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | tasks per batch (one job per batch) |
| `minBatchNum` | `min_batch_num` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | fewest tasks per batch (whole-node jobs) |
| `paraBatchNum` | `para_batch_num` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | batches per slot of a whole-node job |
| `runExtraTasks` | `run_extra_tasks` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | whole-node jobs: shape batches to a multiple of maxJobNum |
| `MatlabLaunchStr` | `matlab_launch_str` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | MATLAB only; ignored (default differs from MATLAB: MATLAB only: accepted and ignored) |
| `PythonLaunchStr` | `python_launch_str` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | how a job starts Python: module loads, environment activation, exports, then the interpreter (empty: the interpreter of this process) (Python only) |

### `imresize3_average` -> `petakit5d.matlabcompat.resize.imresize3_average`

PetaKit5D's ``imresize3_average(im, resampleFactor)``: the single mean of each block of ``resample_factor`` ``(z, y, x)`` voxels; sizes that the factor does not divide are padded with zeros at the end and the edge blocks divided by their voxels inside the image. The result has MATLAB's squeezed shape (a factor that leaves one plane along an axis drops it). A NaN result has the NaN MATLAB's sum gives it (``reduce.sum``): for blocks of up to 43 values along y the first NaN of the block in MATLAB's order (y fastest, then x, z).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `im` | `im` | req | volume |  |  |  | Volume (z, y, x). |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | req | size_yxz |  |  |  | Block size (z, y, x) to average. |

PetaKit5D's ``imresize3_fast(im, outSize, method, outBbox, outClass)``: imresize3 for 'linear' and 'cubic' with its weights and passes, skipping the axes that keep their size.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |

| `method` | `method` | opt |  | `'cubic'` | `'cubic'` |  | 'linear' or 'cubic' (other methods: imresize3's result). |
| `outBbox` | `out_bbox` | opt | bbox | `[]` | `None` |  | Output region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to compute (None: all). |
| `outClass` | `out_class` | opt | class_name | `[]` | `None` |  | MATLAB class to resize in, or its NumPy type (None: the class of im). |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all available). (Python only) |

### `loadTrackSettings` -> `petakit5d.detection.tracking.settings.track_settings`

``loadTrackSettings('Radius', radius, 'GapRadius', gap_radius, 'LinkRadius', link_radius, 'MaxGapLength', max_gap_length)``: a dict with ``costMatrices`` (two dicts of ``funcName`` and ``parameters``), ``gapCloseParam`` and ``kalmanFunctions``, the fields and values (as MATLAB classes in NumPy) of MATLAB's struct (module docstring)

Third-party MATLAB code: its header names an outside author (cmeAnalysis). Its validators are not shown.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Radius` | `radius` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Search radius [min max] of both cost functions (None: GapRadius and LinkRadius). |
| `GapRadius` | `gap_radius` | par |  | `[5,10]` | `(5.0, 10.0)` |  | Search radius [min max] of the gap closing (without Radius). |
| `LinkRadius` | `link_radius` | par |  | `[5,10]` | `(5.0, 10.0)` |  | Search radius [min max] of the linking (without Radius). |
| `MaxGapLength` | `max_gap_length` | par |  | `2` | `2` |  | Longest gap closed, in frames (the time window less one). |
| `CostType` | `cost_type` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` |  | Not ported: 'linear' only (in any case): MATLAB's other values give settings without the cost functions' names, which tracking cannot use. |

### `MIP_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_block`

``MIP_block``: for each batch (``batch_bboxes``, in the zarr), its MIP (or max pooling) along each axis written into that axis's slab zarr (``mip_fullpaths``, ``(z, y, x)``) at the batch's place relative to ``start_coord``; then the empty flag file. Pool sizes: 3 values along the MIP axis, 6 with the pooling across it, 9 with resampling factors. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `MIPFullpaths` | `mip_fullpaths` | req | custom:_paths_from_matlab |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | MIP slab zarrs along z, y and x. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `startCoord` | `start_coord` | req | point1_yxz |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Start (z, y, x) of the region. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pool sizes (z, y, x): along the MIP axis[, across it[, resampling]]. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `N5ToZarr` -> `petakit5d.io.n5.n5_to_zarr`

Convert an N5 dataset (or its box ``bbox``) to a native zarr array ``<folder>/<result_dir_str>/<name>.zarr`` (``N5ToZarr``), in batches of whole chunks through the job framework. ``axes``: the dataset's layout, ``'zyx'`` (standard N5, as the port writes it) or ``'yxz'`` (MATLAB's zarrToN5); it can't be told from the file. An existing result is kept.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `n5Fullpath` | `n5_fullpath` | req |  |  |  |  | N5 dataset (or a folder with a .zgroup: its s0). (renamed) |
| `resultDirStr` | `result_dir_str` | par |  | `'zarr/'` | `'zarr/'` | `@ischar` | Result folder next to the N5 dataset. |

### `N5ToZarrBlock` -> `petakit5d.io.n5.n5_to_zarr_block`

Copy the input boxes of an N5 dataset (layout ``axes``) into the output boxes of an existing native zarr array (``N5ToZarrBlock``), with 65535 set to 0 (``flip_empty_value``), then write the task's flag. The flag's folder must exist (else MATLAB's error: the run is over).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `outFullpath` | `out_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Output zarr (native, existing). |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `inBatchBBoxes` | `in_batch_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Input box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `outBatchBBoxes` | `out_batch_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Output box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `flipEmptyValue` | `flip_empty_value` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Set 65535 (empty regions) to 0. |
| `axes` | `axes` | par |  | `'zyx'` | `'zyx'` |  | Layout of the N5 dataset: 'zyx' (standard N5) or 'yxz' (MATLAB's zarrToN5). (Python only) |

The OTF masked Wiener back projector of a PSF (omw_backprojector_generation): the back projector, the masked Wiener filter, the shifted \|OTF\| and the OTF mask (see the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `psf` | `psf` | req | volume |  |  |  | PSF, a (z, y, x) volume or a (y, x) image. |
| `alpha` | `alpha` | req |  |  |  |  | Wiener parameter. |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | req |  |  |  |  | PSF in the skewed space; None (MATLAB's []): detected. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | Accumulated OTF energy quantile for the OTF mask. |
| `OTFAreaThresh` | `otf_area_thresh` | par |  | `100` | `100` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest component of the OTF mask, in voxels. |

### `plotIntensityCohorts` -> `petakit5d.detection.lifetime.plot_intensity_cohorts`

MATLAB's ``cohorts = plotIntensityCohorts(data, 'AnalysisPath', analysis_path)`` (module docstring; the figure left out): one dict per significance combination of the other channel (all true first), with ``bounds`` (in the first only), ``t``, ``Amin``, ``Aplus`` and ``A`` (channels x cohorts object arrays of rows). Reads the lifetime data files (``lft_data_name``, lifetimeData.mat; it writes them first where they are missing, where MATLAB fails). With one movie, ``A`` is the median of the tracks and ``Amin``, ``Aplus`` their 25th and 75th percentiles (MATLAB's other branch, measured). ``data`` as ``run_lifetime_analysis`` takes it. Registered with MATLAB's interface (names, order, defaults) and the port's own checks of the options; it takes ``analysis_path`` and ``lft_data_name``, every other option at its default only (``NotImplementedError`` otherwise).

Third-party MATLAB code: its header names an outside author (cmeAnalysis). Its validators are not shown.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | `data` | req | same |  |  | (third-party: not shown) | Movies (the structure XR_loadConditionData3D returns). |
| `ch` | `ch` | opt |  | `nCh:-1:1` | `None` |  | Channels (the default only: all). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Not ported: false only. |

### `processFFCorrectionFrame` -> `petakit5d.flatfield.correction.flatfield_correct_file`

Flat-field correction of a frame in memory with the flat field and the background read from tiff files (``processFFCorrectionFrame``); the rest as ``flatfield_correct_volume``. The files are read at every call, as MATLAB reads them.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frame` | `frame` | req | volume |  |  |  | Data, (z, y, x) or (y, x). |
| `LSImage` | `ls_image` | req | same |  |  |  | Tiff file of the flat-field image (or stack). |
| `BackgroundImage` | `background_image` | req |  |  |  |  | Tiff file of the camera background image. |
| `constOffset` | `const_offset` | req | same |  |  |  | If not empty, added after the correction instead of the background. |
| `LowerLimit` | `lower_limit` | opt |  | `0.4` | `0.4` |  | Lowest value of the normalized flat field. |
| `removeFFImBackground` | `remove_ff_im_background` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Subtract the background from the flat-field image. |
| `LSRescale` | `ls_rescale` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Divide the flat field by its maximum. |
| `castDataType` | `cast_data_type` | opt |  | `true` | `True` |  | Cast the result to the data's class; otherwise single. |
| `out` | `out` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Array to write the result into (reused over frames). (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the correction (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `processStitchBlock` -> `petakit5d.stitch.block.process_stitch_block`

``processStitchBlock``: blend the batches ``batch_inds`` of the stitched volume (size ``im_size``, batches ``batch_size``) into ``stitch_fullname`` and write the task's flag (see the module). The batches' tiles come from ``per_block_info_fullname`` (JSON, or ``.mat``), else ``stitch_block_info`` (``{k: [TileBlock, ...]}`` for the task's k-th batch, MATLAB's struct); the tiles' paths from ``block_info_fullname``, else ``tile_fns``. Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based) of the task. |
| `BlockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The block information file (tile paths). |
| `PerBlockInfoFullname` | `per_block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The task's per-block information file. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `stitchFullname` | `stitch_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The stitched zarr (made before), written into. |
| `stitchBlockInfo` | `stitch_block_info` | opt | custom:_stitch_block_info_from_matlab | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isstruct(x)` | The batches' block information (without a per-block file). |
| `tileFns` | `tile_fns` | opt |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Tile zarr files (without a block file). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo a task whose flag exists. |
| `imSize` | `im_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Size (z, y, x) of the stitched volume. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par | class_name | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `@ischar` | Class of the stitched volume (or its NumPy type). |
| `BlendMethod` | `blend_method` | par |  | `'feather'` | `'feather'` | `@ischar` | 'none', 'max', 'mean', 'median' or 'feather'. |
| `BorderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Border of the batches (z, y, x). |
| `imdistFullpaths` | `imdist_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Distance maps (feather). |
| `imdistFileIdx` | `imdist_file_idx` | par | index1 | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | The distance map of each tile. |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pooling of the distance maps (z, y, x). |
| `weightDegree` | `weight_degree` | par |  | `10` | `10` | `@isnumeric` | Power of the distance weights (pooled maps). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the batches' z MIPs in the flag. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the kernels, reads and writes (None: the CPUs this process may use). (Python only) |

### `psf_gen_new` -> `petakit5d.decon.psf.psf_gen`

Clean, center and resample a raw PSF (psf_gen_new): the background removed, the peak at the FFT center ``size // 2``, z resampled to the data's step (see the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `dz_data` | `dz_data` | req |  |  |  |  | z step of the data the PSF is resampled to. |
| `medFactor` | `med_factor` | opt |  | `1.5` | `1.5` |  | Factor of the median subtracted by the 'median' method. |
| `PSFGenMethod` | `psf_gen_method` | opt |  | `'masked'` | `'masked'` |  | Background removal: 'masked' or 'median'. |

### `readtiff` -> `petakit5d.io.tiff.read_tiff`

Read a tiff file as a ``(z, y, x)`` C-ordered array (MATLAB ``readtiff``).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` | Tiff file. |

### `readtiff_parallel` -> `petakit5d.io.tiff.read_tiff_parallel`

MATLAB ``readtiff_parallel``: the slices listed in ``range`` (0-based indices, any order, repeats allowed) stacked along z, through cpp-tiff in runs of consecutive slices; all slices without it. One slice comes back as an image ``(y, x)``. ``info`` is accepted and not used.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  |  | Tiff file. |
| `range` | `range` | opt | index1 | `[]` | `None` |  | Slices to read, 0-based, in any order (empty: all). |
| `info` | `info` | opt |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isstruct` | Accepted; not used (the reader takes the file's own tags). |
| `ShowWaitbar` | `show_waitbar` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's error (known issue: its waitbar loop reads through a Tiff it never makes). |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `readzarr` -> `petakit5d.io.zarr.read_zarr`

Read a zarr array, or its box ``input_bbox`` (``(z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1)``, 0-based, end-exclusive; ``None`` ends are open, NaN and infinite values are refused with MATLAB's ``MATLAB:validators:mustBeFinite``), as a ``(z, y, x)`` C-ordered array (MATLAB ``readzarr``); one slice comes back as an image ``(y, x)``.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` | Zarr array. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric, mustBeFinite}` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to read (None ends: open; empty: all). |
| `sparseData` | `sparse_data` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumericOrLogical}` | Accepted; results are the same. |
| `axes` | `axes` | par |  | `'auto'` | `'auto'` |  | Layout of the array: 'auto' (MATLAB's when its order is F), 'zyx' or 'yxz'. (Python only) |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |
| `out` | `out` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Array or ReadBuffer to read into (reused over reads). (Python only) |

### `resampleZarrBlock` -> `petakit5d.tools.resample.resample_zarr_block`

``resampleZarrBlock``: each batch of the result zarr ``ds_fullpath`` (``batch_size``, numbered with y fastest, then x, then z), resampled from the input region it maps to plus the border, in the data's class, then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `dsFullpath` | `ds_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | req | resample_yxz |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Factor (z, y, x) or one number; above 1 shrinks. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the input that was resampled. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) in the result (None: twice block_size). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `borderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Border (z, y, x) read around a batch, rounded up to a multiple of the factor. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@ischar` | 'linear', 'cubic', 'nearest', 'max' or 'mean'. |

### `resaveZarrBlock` -> `petakit5d.io.resave.resave_zarr_block`

Copy the input boxes ``batch_b_boxes`` of a zarr array into the output boxes ``region_b_boxes`` of an existing native array (``resaveZarrBlock``), then write the task's flag. The flag's folder must exist (else MATLAB's error: the run is over).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers of the task (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr (either layout). |
| `resultFullpath` | `result_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Output zarr (native, existing). |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_b_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Input box of each batch. |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_b_boxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Output box of each batch. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |

### `RLdecon` -> `petakit5d.decon.rl_file.rl_decon_file`

Deconvolve a file (or ``rawdata``) and deskew and rotate the result (RLdecon): see the module. Arrays are (z, y, x); boxes, sizes and MIP axes in (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFn` | `input_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | Input file (tiff or zarr); '' for data in rawdata. |
| `outputFn` | `output_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | Result file; '' to only return the result. |
| `PSFfn` | `psf_fn` | req |  |  |  |  | PSF file (tiff, or a .mat file with the variable psf). (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `rawdata` | `rawdata` | par | volume | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Data (z, y, x) used instead of reading inputFn. |
| `nTapering` | `n_tapering` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Edge tapering in x-y (edgetaper; not ported). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Results in uint16 (else single). |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan data (rotation without deskew). |
| `Deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deskew the result. |
| `Rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Rotate the result (with the deskew for sample scans). |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deskew and rotate in one step. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the data in z first. |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation. |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Crop of the separate deskew. |
| `xStepThresh` | `x_step_thresh` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | x step above which the combined path interpolates. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background ([]: 0 for 'simplified' and 'omw'). |
| `Resample` | `resample` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Resample factor of the combined deskew and rotation (z, y, x). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'omw'` | `'omw'` | `@(x) any(strcmp(x, {'original', 'simplified', 'omw', 'cudagen'}))` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Fixed number of iterations (saved in the info file). |
| `errThresh` | `err_thresh` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Error threshold (saved in the info file). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save results as zarr. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Block size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `ErodeMaskfile` | `erode_maskfile` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Eroded mask zarr used instead of the erosion. |
| `deconBbox` | `decon_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the result. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the iterations' estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every saveStep iterations (debug). |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | True: on a GPU (an error if none is usable); False: the CPU; None: a GPU if one is usable. |
| `save3Dstack` | `save_3d_stack` | par | same | `[true,true,true]` | `(True, True, True)` | `@(x) islogical(x) && (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 3)` | Save the (decon, DS, DSR) stacks. (renamed) |
| `mipAxis` | `mip_axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && numel(x) == 3` | MIP axes (z, y, x). |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate the PSF (psf_gen) or use it as it is. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | uuid of the temporary names. |
| `overlapFcn` | `overlap_fcn` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x, 'function_handle')` | Function run on the CPU while the GPU deconvolves; its output is returned. |

### `RLdecon_for_zarr_block` -> `petakit5d.decon.block.decon_zarr_block`

``RLdecon_for_zarr_block``: deconvolve the batches of a large zarr file into the result zarr and write the task's flag (see the module). Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `psfFullpath` | `psf_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | PSF file. |
| `deconFullpath` | `decon_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch (with its border) in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region in the result. (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Result in uint16 (else single). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `-32.45` | `-32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background. |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of each batch's mask. |
| `maskFullpaths` | `mask_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | 2D masks (xy, xz, yz) of the regions to decon. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'simplified'` | `'simplified'` | `@ischar` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; the blocks fix the iterations (as in MATLAB). |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deconvolve on a GPU (an error if none is usable). |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate the PSF (psf_gen) or use it as it is. |

### `RLdecon_large_in_memory` -> `petakit5d.decon.large.decon_large_in_memory`

Deconvolve a file that fits in memory batch by batch (RLdecon_large_in_memory): see the module. Sizes are (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpaths` | `frame_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | File to deconvolve (tiff or zarr). |
| `deconFullpath` | `psf_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | PSF file (the second argument; MATLAB's inputParser names it deconFullpath). (renamed) |
| `psfFullpath` | `decon_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result file (the third argument; MATLAB's inputParser names it psfFullpath). (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Result in uint16 (else single). |
| `Rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (the batches are only deconvolved). |
| `Deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Border PSF at dz sind(SkewAngle); the batches aren't deskewed. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `-32.45` | `-32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the data in z first. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background ([]: 100). |
| `EdgeSoften` | `edge_soften` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `zEdgeSoften` | `z_edge_soften` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `zFlip` | `z_flip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |

| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `8` | `8` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of the mask of nonzero data. |
| `ErodeMaskfile` | `erode_maskfile` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Eroded mask tiff multiplied with the result instead. |
| `SaveMaskfile` | `save_maskfile` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the eroded mask (Masks/<name>_eroded.tif). |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `maskFullpaths` | `mask_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | 2D masks (xy, xz, yz) of the regions to decon. |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as zarr. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'simplified'` | `'simplified'` | `@ischar` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Fixed number of iterations. |
| `errThresh` | `err_thresh` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of a zarr result (None: writezarr's). |
| `Overlap` | `overlap` | par | same | `200` | `200` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `CPUMaxMem` | `cpu_max_mem` | par |  | `500` | `500` | `@isnumeric` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | uuid of the temporary names. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (the batches run without debug). |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate the PSF (psf_gen) or use it as it is. |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | True: on a GPU (an error if none is usable); False: the CPU; None: a GPU if usable. |

### `RLdecon_large_in_place` -> `petakit5d.decon.large.decon_large_in_place`

Deconvolve a large zarr file in blocks, each written into the result (RLdecon_large_in_ place): see the module. Sizes are (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpaths` | `frame_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Zarr file to deconvolve. |
| `pixelSize` | `pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan step (um). |
| `deconPath` | `decon_path` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| isempty(x)` | Result folder. |
| `PSF` | `psf` | req | same |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| isempty(x)` | PSF file. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Result in uint16 (else single). |
| `Deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Border PSF at dz sind(SkewAngle). |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `-32.45` | `-32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Passed to the blocks, which ignore it (as in MATLAB). |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Background ([]: 100). |
| `dzPSF` | `dz_psf` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | z step of the PSF (um). |
| `DeconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Number of iterations. |
| `RLMethod` | `rl_method` | par |  | `'simplified'` | `'simplified'` | `@ischar` | 'simplified', 'omw', 'original' or 'cudagen'. |
| `wienerAlpha` | `wiener_alpha` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener alpha of the OMW back projector. |
| `OTFCumThresh` | `otf_cum_thresh` | par |  | `0.9` | `0.9` | `@isnumeric` | OTF cumulative sum threshold (OMW). |
| `hannWinBounds` | `hann_win_bounds` | par |  | `[0.8,1]` | `(0.8, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Apodization range of the distance matrix (OMW). |
| `skewed` | `skewed` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| islogical(x)` | Decon in skewed space (None: automatic). |
| `fixIter` | `fix_iter` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Passed to the blocks, which fix the iterations anyway. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x), in whole chunks. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `dampFactor` | `damp_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Damp factor of the result. |
| `scaleFactor` | `scale_factor` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Scale factor of the result ([]: 1). |
| `deconOffset` | `decon_offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Offset of the result. |
| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges of each batch's mask. |
| `maskFullpaths` | `mask_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | 2D masks (xy, xz, yz) of the regions to decon. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks on a local process pool. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks (CPU) and MIPs too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job (4 for GPU jobs). |
| `GPUJob` | `gpu_job` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deconvolve the blocks on GPUs. |

### `rotateTracks3D` -> `petakit5d.detection.processing.rotate_tracks`

``rotateTracks3D(data, 'Crop', false, ...)``: ``<results>/RotatedTracks.mat`` (v7.3, ``tracks``) from ``<results>/ProcessedTracks.mat``, the geometry from the first DS frame of channel 1 and the movie's ``angle`` and ``zAniso`` (stage scans: measured). A v7.3 file of two or more tracks is rotated in a copy (``_rotate_v73``), any other file through ``rotated_tracks``. ``data`` as ``run_track_processing`` takes it (a ``Condition`` too).

Third-party MATLAB code: its header names an outside author (cmeAnalysis), with lab edits. Its validators are not shown.

| `matlab_quirks` | `matlab_quirks` | par |  | `false` | `False` |  | MATLAB's rotation of a reversed scan's tracks by the forward angle (W20). (Python only) |

### `runLifetimeAnalysis3D` -> `petakit5d.detection.lifetime.run_lifetime_analysis`

MATLAB's ``lftRes = runLifetimeAnalysis3D(data, ...)`` (module docstring; the figures are left out). ``data``: the condition's movies (``Condition``, a struct array, dicts with MATLAB's fields: source, framerate, movieLength, channels). The options the port takes: ``analysis_path``, ``processed_tracks``, ``lifetime_data``, ``overwrite`` and ``display`` (no figures either way); the others at their defaults only. Returns lftRes with MATLAB's fields and order; writes lifetimeData.mat per movie and Parameters.mat in the first movie's analysis folder.

### `saveMIP_tiff` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.save_mip_tiff`

The MIPs of a tiff file (or of its box) along the flagged axes, cast to ``dtype``, as ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` in MATLAB's orientation (``saveMIP_tiff``); nothing if ``mip_fullname`` exists. Each MIP is written under a temporary name and moved into place.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullname` | `frame_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Tiff file. |
| `MIPFullname` | `mip_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | MIP file name (<name>_MIP_z.tif); skipped if it exists. |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par | class_name | `'uint16'` | `'uint16'` | `@ischar` | Class of the MIPs (or its NumPy type). |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |

### `saveMIP_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.save_mip_zarr`

The MIPs of a zarr file (or of its box) along the flagged axes, cast to ``dtype`` (the zarr's class if empty), as ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` in MATLAB's orientation (``saveMIP_zarr``). The image is read in z slabs of whole chunk layers (at least 64 chunks a read) with a running max along z, so it is not held whole. Each MIP is written under a temporary name and moved into place.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullname` | `zarr_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Zarr file. |
| `MIPFullname` | `mip_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | MIP file name (<name>_MIP_z.tif). |
| `dtype` | `dtype` | par | class_name | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x)` | Class of the MIPs, or its NumPy type (empty: the zarr's class). |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@(x) isvector(x) \|\| numel(x) == 3` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |

### `simRecon` -> `petakit5d.sim.recon.sim_recon_volume`

A super-resolved SIM volume from the raw phases of a deskewed volume and the OTF (``simRecon``): ``(nz, 2 (ny // 2) + ny, 2 (nx // 2) + nx)`` (twice the lateral size), single (double with ``gpu_precision='double'``). With ``use_gpu`` it runs on a usable GPU (CuPy), else on the CPU; MATLAB's ``useGPU`` is the same (true with a GPU).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | `data` | req | volume |  |  |  | Raw phases of a deskewed volume (nz * phases * orientations, y, x), phases interleaved. |
| `otf` | `otf` | req | volume |  |  |  | OTF orders (norientations, norders, z, y, x), complex (sim_psf_to_otf). |
| `islattice` | `islattice` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Lattice light sheet data (else 3-beam SIM). |
| `NA_det` | `na_det` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@isnumeric` | Detection NA. |
| `NA_ext` | `na_ext` | par |  | `0.55` | `0.55` | `@isnumeric` | Excitation NA. |
| `nimm` | `nimm` | par |  | `1.33` | `1.33` | `@isnumeric` | Refractive index of the immersion medium. |
| `wvl_em` | `wvl_em` | par |  | `0.605` | `0.605` | `@isnumeric` | Emission wavelength in um. |
| `wvl_ext` | `wvl_ext` | par |  | `0.56` | `0.56` | `@isnumeric` | Excitation wavelength in um. |
| `w` | `w` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener coefficient for regularization. |
| `wVer` | `w_ver` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener filter: 1 each order's OTF, 0 the sum of the shifted OTFs, 2 that sum after. |
| `apodize` | `apodize` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Apodize the final spectrum. |
| `normalize_orientations` | `normalize_orientations` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Normalize the total intensity per orientation. |
| `perdecomp` | `perdecomp` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Periodic-plus-smooth decomposition against edge effects. |
| `edgeTaper` | `edge_taper` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Taper the edges (Tukey window) against edge effects. |
| `edgeTaperVal` | `edge_taper_val` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | Roll-off of the Tukey window. |
| `nphases` | `nphases` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Phases. |
| `norders` | `norders` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Information orders. |
| `norientations` | `norientations` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Pattern orientations. |
| `lattice_period` | `lattice_period` | par |  | `1.2021` | `1.2021` | `@isnumeric` | Lattice period in um (the coarsest). |
| `lattice_angle` | `lattice_angle` | par |  | `1.5707963267948966` | `1.5707963267948966` | `@isnumeric` | Angle of the pattern modulation per orientation (rad). |
| `phase_step` | `phase_step` | par |  | `0.232` | `0.232` | `@isnumeric` | Phase step in um. |
| `pxl_dim_data` | `pxl_dim_data` | par | size_yxz | `[0.11,0.11,0.16074803849369898]` | `(0.16074803849369898, 0.11, 0.11)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size of the data (z, y, x) in um. |
| `pxl_dim_PSF` | `pxl_dim_psf` | par | size_yxz | `[0.11,0.11,0.10716535899579933]` | `(0.10716535899579933, 0.11, 0.11)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size of the PSF (z, y, x) in um. |

### `simReconFrame` -> `petakit5d.sim.file.sim_recon_file`

SIM reconstructions of files of raw phases (``simReconFrame``) into ``<folder>/<results_dir_name>/<name>_recon.tif`` and its z MIP; returns the last volume reconstructed ``(nz, 2 ny, 2 nx)`` (None when every file was skipped). ``otf`` is an OTF array or the PSF's tiff file (``sim_psf_to_otf`` with the same values).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpaths` | `frame_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Tiff or zarr files of raw phases. |

| `resultsDirName` | `results_dir_name` | par |  | `'sim_recon'` | `'sim_recon'` | `@ischar` | Result folder next to each file. |
| `islattice` | `islattice` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Lattice light sheet data (else 3-beam SIM). |
| `NA_det` | `na_det` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@isnumeric` | Detection NA. |
| `NA_ext` | `na_ext` | par |  | `0.55` | `0.55` | `@isnumeric` | Excitation NA. |
| `nimm` | `nimm` | par |  | `1.33` | `1.33` | `@isnumeric` | Refractive index of the immersion medium. |
| `wvl_em` | `wvl_em` | par |  | `0.605` | `0.605` | `@isnumeric` | Emission wavelength in um. |
| `wvl_ext` | `wvl_ext` | par |  | `0.56` | `0.56` | `@isnumeric` | Excitation wavelength in um. |
| `w` | `w` | par |  | `0.005` | `0.005` | `@isnumeric` | Wiener coefficient for regularization. |
| `apodize` | `apodize` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Apodize the final spectrum. |
| `DS` | `ds` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The data are deskewed (else chunks span x on the GPU). |
| `nphases` | `nphases` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Phases. |
| `norders` | `norders` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Information orders. |
| `norientations` | `norientations` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Pattern orientations. |
| `lattice_period` | `lattice_period` | par |  | `1.2021` | `1.2021` | `@isnumeric` | Lattice period in um (the coarsest). |
| `lattice_angle` | `lattice_angle` | par |  | `1.5707963267948966` | `1.5707963267948966` | `@isnumeric` | Angle of the pattern modulation per orientation (rad). |
| `phase_step` | `phase_step` | par |  | `0.232` | `0.232` | `@isnumeric` | Phase step in um. |
| `pxl_dim_data` | `pxl_dim_data` | par | size_yxz | `[0.11,0.11,0.16074803849369898]` | `(0.16074803849369898, 0.11, 0.11)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size of the data (z, y, x) in um. |
| `pxl_dim_PSF` | `pxl_dim_psf` | par | size_yxz | `[0.11,0.11,0.10716535899579933]` | `(0.10716535899579933, 0.11, 0.11)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size of the PSF (z, y, x) in um. |
| `Background` | `background` | par | same | `105` | `105` | `@isnumeric` | Background subtracted from data and PSF. |
| `normalize_orientations` | `normalize_orientations` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Normalize the total intensity per orientation. |
| `perdecomp` | `perdecomp` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Periodic-plus-smooth decomposition against edge effects. |
| `edgeTaper` | `edge_taper` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Taper the edges (Tukey window) against edge effects. |
| `edgeTaperVal` | `edge_taper_val` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | Roll-off of the Tukey window. |
| `useGPU` | `use_gpu` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run on a GPU if one is usable. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace existing results. |
| `Save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as uint16 (else single). |
| `gpuPrecision` | `gpu_precision` | par |  | `'single'` | `'single'` | `@ischar` | Precision of GPU arrays and phase ramps: 'single', 'double'. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the raw pages along z first. |

| `zEdgeSoften` | `z_edge_soften` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `Crop` | `crop` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
