| `EdgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Radius of the disk that erodes the data's mask (0: none). |
| `ErodeBefore` | `erode_before` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Apply the eroded mask to the raw data. |
| `ErodeAfter` | `erode_after` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Apply the eroded mask, resized, to the result. |
| `ErodeMaskfile` | `erode_maskfile` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | An existing mask file turns the erosion off (MATLAB's). |
| `SaveMaskfile` | `save_maskfile` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the eroded mask under <results>/Masks/. |
| `ChunkSize` | `chunk_size` | par | size_yxz | `[250,250,250]` | `(250, 250, 250)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of one phase. |
| `Overlap` | `overlap` | par | same | `10` | `10` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk overlap in voxels. |
| `maxSubVolume` | `max_sub_volume` | par |  | `300000000` | `300000000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest chunk in voxels. |
| `CPUMaxMem` | `cpu_max_mem` | par |  | `500` | `500` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `masterCPU` | `master_cpu` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Not used. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary files (empty: a new one). |
| `maxTrialNum` | `max_trial_num` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `unitWaitTime` | `unit_wait_time` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `intThresh` | `int_thresh` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | A chunk is reconstructed if its sum is above this. |
| `occThresh` | `occ_thresh` | par |  | `0.8` | `0.8` | `@isnumeric` | ... and its nonzero fraction at least this. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all available). (Python only) |
| `fft_backend` | `fft_backend` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | CPU FFT backend (None: the default). (Python only) |

### `stitch_global_grid_assignment_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.stitch.assignment.global_grid_assignment`

The grid assignment of a group of tiles as a task: reads the group's problem from a .mat file (MATLAB's form: ``nF_g``, ``max_xcorr_mat_g``, ``absolute_shift_mat_g``, ``overlap_matrix_g``, ``max_allow_shift_g``, ``tileIdx_g``) and writes ``d_shift_g`` to ``output_fullpath`` (through a temporary file); an existing result is kept.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFullpath` | `input_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The group's optimization information (.mat). |
| `outputFullpath` | `output_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The result file (.mat, with d_shift_g). |
| `xcorr_thresh` | `xcorr_thresh` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isscalar(x)` | Pairs below this correlation are not fitted. |
| `axisWeight` | `axis_weight` | req | size_yxz |  |  | `@(x) isvector(x)` | Weights of the neighbors along tile index z, y and x. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Id of the temporary result file. |

### `stitch_organize_block_info` -> `petakit5d.stitch.blockinfo.organize_block_info`

``stitch_organize_block_info``: the per-block information of batches ``block_inds[0]`` to ``block_inds[-1]`` (0-based) from the block information file, written as one JSON file per ``task_size`` batches (``stitch_block_info_blocks_<s>_<t>.json``, 1-based numbers; a temporary name, then a rename), then the task's flag (``blockInds``, ``t1``). Nothing is done if the flag exists (``overwrite`` removes it). Returns whether the task ran.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blockInds` | `block_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | First and last batch (0-based) of the task. |
| `taskSize` | `task_size` | req | same |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batches per blending task (one JSON file each). |
| `BlockInfoFullname` | `block_info_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | The block information file. |
| `PerBlockInfoPath` | `per_block_info_path` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Folder of the per-block information files. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when the task is done. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo a task whose flag exists. |
| `BorderSize` | `border_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Border of the batches (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary files (empty: a new one). |

### `stitch_process_zarr_tile` -> `petakit5d.stitch.tiles.process_zarr_tile`

Process a zarr tile (stitch_process_zarr_tile): read it (its box ``input_bbox``), flip it in z, crop it to ``tile_out_bbox``, resample it to ``round(size ./ resample)`` (MATLAB's ``imresize3``), apply ``usr_fcn``, cast it back to its class and write it as a native zarr with chunks of ``block_size`` (cut to the result's size). An existing result is kept (``overwrite``: replaced first). A ``frame`` (data in memory) is processed instead of the tile.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inputFilename` | `input_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Zarr tile (or a list whose first is read). |
| `zarrFilename` | `zarr_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Zarr result. |
| `frame` | `frame` | opt | volume | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Data in memory instead of the tile. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,250]` | `(250, 500, 500)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) (Zarr v3 with shards). |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Store an image as one z slice ([1, ny, nx]). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the tile in z. |
| `resample` | `resample` | par | resample_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling factors (z, y, x) or (z, xy) (None: none). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to read. |
| `tileOutBbox` | `tile_out_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to keep after the flip. |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | par |  | `'lz4'` | `'lz4'` | `@ischar` | Zarr compressor. |
| `usrFcn` | `usr_fcn` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x)` | Tile function applied after the resampling (MATLAB code, callable or import path). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary result (empty: a new one). |

### `tiffToZarr` -> `petakit5d.io.convert.tiff_to_zarr`

Convert a tiff file (its partial files joined along z, or ``frame``, a ``(z, y, x)`` array) to a native zarr array (``tiffToZarr``), with the flip in z, the crop boxes, resampling and the user function in MATLAB's order, and the result cast back to the input's class. An existing result is kept (``overwrite``: replaced first).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tifFilename` | `tif_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Tiff file, or its partial files. |
| `zarrFilename` | `zarr_filename` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Zarr result. |
| `frame` | `frame` | opt | volume | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Data in memory to convert instead (None: read the tiff). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,250]` | `(250, 500, 500)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Shard size (z, y, x) (Zarr v3 with shards). |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Store an image as one z slice ([1, ny, nx]). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the volume in z. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling factors (z, y, x) or (z, xy) (None: none). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to read. |
| `tileOutBbox` | `tile_out_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to keep after the flip. |
| `readWholeTiff` | `read_whole_tiff` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Read the tiff at once (else by z blocks). |
| `compressor` | `compressor` | par |  | `'zstd'` | `'zstd'` | `@ischar` | Zarr compressor. |
| `usrFcn` | `usr_fcn` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isa(x,'function_handle') \|\| ischar(x) \|\| isstring(x)` | User function applied last (callable, import path or record). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary result (empty: a new one). |

### `unmix_channels_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_block`

``unmix_channels_block``: the linear unmixing of each batch (``batch_bboxes``) of the channels' zarr files, written into the same box of the result zarr ``unmix_fullpath`` in its class; then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpaths` | `zarr_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | Zarr files of the channels. |
| `unmixFullpath` | `unmix_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `unmix_channels_gaussian_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.unmix.unmix_channels_gaussian_block`

``unmix_channels_gaussian_block``: each batch with its border (``batch_bboxes``), each channel with a nonzero sigma filtered (``imgaussfilt3`` in single), unmixed, its region (``local_bboxes``) written into ``region_bboxes`` of the result zarr in its class; then the flag. Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpaths` | `zarr_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) iscell(x)` | Zarr files of the channels. |
| `unmixFullpath` | `unmix_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `unmixFactors` | `unmix_factors` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Unmixing factor of each channel. |
| `unmixSigmas` | `unmix_sigmas` | req | custom:_sigmas_in |  |  | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Sigma of each channel: a scalar or (z, y, x); 0 for no filter. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch with its border. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region in the result. (renamed) |
| `localBBoxes` | `local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each region inside its batch. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `writetiff` -> `petakit5d.io.tiff.write_tiff`

Write an image ``(y, x)`` or a volume ``(z, y, x)`` as a tiff file, one page per z (MATLAB ``writetiff``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `img` | `img` | req | volume |  |  | `{mustBeNumeric}` | Image (y, x) or volume (z, y, x). |
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` | Tiff file. |
| `Compression` | `compression` | par |  | `'lzw'` | `'lzw'` | `(1, :) char {mustBeMember(options.Compression, {'none', 'lzw', 'zstd'})}` | Compression: 'lzw', 'zstd' or 'none'. |
| `Mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'parallel'` | `'parallel'` | `(1, :) char {mustBeMember(options.Mode, {'parallel', 'libtiff', 'imwrite'})}` | MATLAB's writer ('parallel', 'libtiff', 'imwrite'); every mode writes cpp-tiff's BigTIFF (decision 0004). |
| `groupWrite` | `group_write` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Make the file group-writable (not on Windows). |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `writezarr` -> `petakit5d.io.zarr.write_zarr`

Write a volume ``(z, y, x)`` or an image ``(y, x)`` as a native zarr array (MATLAB ``writezarr``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | `data` | req | volume |  |  | `{mustBeNumeric}` | Image (y, x) or volume (z, y, x). |
| `filepath` | `filepath` | req |  |  |  | `char` | Zarr array. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `(1, 1) logical` | Replace an existing array (without bbox). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[500,500,500]` | `(500, 500, 500)` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of a new array. |
| `shardSize` | `shard_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Inner chunk size (z, y, x) of a new sharded array (empty: no sharding). |
| `zarrSubSize` | `zarr_sub_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Subfolders (z, y, x) of the chunk files of a new array. |
| `expand2dDim` | `expand_2d_dim` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Accepted; an image is stored as (1, y, x), as in MATLAB. |
| `groupWrite` | `group_write` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Make a new array group-writable (not on Windows). |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `(1, :) {mustBeNumeric}` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the array to write. |
| `sparseData` | `sparse_data` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `(1, :) logical` | Leave out chunks that are all zero. |
| `dimSeparator` | `dim_separator` | par |  | `'.'` | `'.'` | `char` | Chunk key separator of a new array: '.' or '/'. |
| `create` | `create` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `logical` | Create a missing array (else an error); flags of an array as MATLAB's if: all true. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `(1, 1) logical` | Write chunks to temporary names, then rename them. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `writeZarrBlock` -> `petakit5d.io.zarr.write_zarr_block`

Write one chunk of a native zarr array by its chunk index ``(iz, iy, ix)``, 0-based (MATLAB ``writeZarrBlock``, on the array's path instead of a ``blockedImage``); data past the array's edge is cut off. ``level`` and ``mode`` are accepted for compatibility.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bim` | `file_path` | req |  |  |  |  | Zarr array (its path, in place of MATLAB's blockedImage). (renamed) |
| `blockSub` | `block_sub` | req | point1_yxz |  |  |  | Chunk index (z, y, x), 0-based. |
| `data` | `data` | req | volume |  |  |  | The chunk's data (z, y, x). |
| `level` | `level` | opt |  | `1` | `1` |  | Accepted; zarr arrays have one level. |
| `Mode` | `mode` | opt |  | `'w'` | `'w'` |  | Accepted; both of MATLAB's modes write the chunk cut to the array. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `XR_bioformats_to_tiff_or_zarr_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.io.bioformats.bioformats_to_tiff_or_zarr_dataset`

Convert the ND2 and CZI files of datasets (those that match a channel pattern and a data format) to one tiff or zarr per series and channel in ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/`` (``XR_bioformats_to_tiff_or_zarr_wrapper``, without Bio-Formats: the readers of the ``formats`` extra), one task per file through the job framework (in this process unless ``parse_cluster``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'tiffs'` | `'tiffs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'.nd2','.czi'}` | `('.nd2', '.czi')` | `@iscell` | File name patterns of the files to convert. |
| `nChannels` | `n_channels` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | Channels the planes of each series are dealt to. |
| `dataFormats` | `data_formats` | par |  | `{'.nd2','.czi'}` | `('.nd2', '.czi')` | `@iscell` | File formats to convert (.nd2 and .czi are supported). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Write zarr (else tiff). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Zarr chunk size (z, y, x). |
| `overWrite` | `over_write` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace existing results. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary results (empty: a new one). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `false` | `False` |  | Run the files as Slurm jobs. (Python only) |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` |  | This process converts files too. (Python only) |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` |  | CPUs of a file's job. (Python only) |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` |  | Folder of the job logs. (Python only) |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` |  | Cluster configuration file. (Python only) |

### `XR_chromatic_shift_correction_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.chromatic.chromatic_shift_correction_dataset`

Align the channels of the datasets by integer voxel offsets (``XR_chromatic_shift_correction_data_wrapper``): with ``chromatic_offset`` (rows ``(z, y, x)``, one per channel pattern) or, without, the offsets measured from ``psf_fullpaths`` (``chromatic_shift_estimation``, saved in ``<first result folder>/measurement/``). Each file is cropped to the ``mode``'s region by a ``crop_file`` task through the job framework (large zarr files in batches), its result in ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/``.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `chromaticOffset` | `chromatic_offset` | par | custom:_offsets_in | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Offsets in voxels, a row (z, y, x) per channel (None: measured from the PSFs). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Chromatic_Shift_Corrected'` | `'Chromatic_Shift_Corrected'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'valid'` | `'valid'` | `@(x) ischar(x) && any(strcmpi(x, {'valid', 'same', 'full'}))` | Output region, in any case: 'valid' (overlap), 'same' (input size) or 'full'. |
| `padValue` | `pad_value` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Value of the voxels without data. |
| `newOrigin` | `new_origin` | par | point1_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Origin (z, y, x) of the 'same' region in the scene (None: centered on the overlap). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA','CamB'}` | `('CamA', 'CamB')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers, one per channel. |
| `psfFullpaths` | `psf_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | PSF files, one per channel (for measured offsets). |
| `maxOffset` | `max_offset` | par | size_yxz | `[20,20,20]` | `(20, 20, 20)` | `@isnumeric` | Largest offset (z, y, x) of the measurement. |
| `cropLength` | `crop_length` | par | size_yxz | `[0,0,0]` | `(0, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxels (z, y, x) cut from each side of the PSFs for the measurement. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large files: zarr files cropped in batches into zarr, tiff files in memory. |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the results as zarr. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `3` | `3` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Debug mode (not used). |

### `XR_chromatic_shift_estimation` -> `petakit5d.tools.chromatic.chromatic_shift_estimation`

The shifts (rows ``(z, y, x)``, the first channel's 0) of the channels' PSFs against the first (``XR_chromatic_shift_estimation``), each the offset of the cropped PSF in the first at the peak of their normalized cross-correlation within ``max_offset`` of the crop, less the crop. With ``result_fullpath`` the paths, bounds, shifts and peaks are saved there (MATLAB's ``.mat``, ``[y x z]`` values; published by one rename).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `psfFullpaths` | `psf_fullpaths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | PSF files, one per channel. |
| `resultFullpath` | `result_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Result .mat file of the measurement (empty: not saved). |
| `maxOffset` | `max_offset` | par | size_yxz | `[30,30,30]` | `(30, 30, 30)` | `@isnumeric` | Largest shift (z, y, x) searched, around the crop. |
| `cropLength` | `crop_length` | par | size_yxz | `[0,0,0]` | `(0, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Voxels (z, y, x) cut from each side of the PSFs after the first. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The PSFs are zarr. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Debug mode (not used). |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the NCC (None: all available). (Python only) |

### `XR_correctXZoffsetData3D` -> `petakit5d.detection.offsets.correct_xz_offset_data`

``XR_correctXZoffsetData3D(data, 'Overwrite', overwrite, 'DS', ds, 'sCMOSCameraFlip', flip)``: line the channels' DS frames up in x and z (module docstring). ``data`` is the condition (``condition.Movie`` items); it is returned unchanged. Registered with the lab's interface and validators.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | `data` | req | custom:_movies_from_matlab |  |  | `@isstruct` | The condition (load_condition_data's movies). |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Estimate and pad again the movies whose offsets exist. |
| `DS` | `ds` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Use the deskewed frames as the estimate's input images. |
| `sCMOSCameraFlip` | `s_cmos_camera_flip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | sCMOS camera flip of the frames read. |

### `XR_crop_block` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_block`

``XR_crop_block``: each batch (``batch_bboxes``, boxes of the input zarr that may reach outside it: zeros there) written into its region (``region_bboxes``) of the result zarr ``crop_fullpath``; then the flag: empty, or with the MIPs of the blocks for MIP slabs of ``mip_batch_size`` (``save_block_mips``). Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``) or if the writer of the crop has ended (``writers.orphaned``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `cropFullpath` | `crop_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `BatchBBoxes` | `batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input. (renamed) |
| `RegionBBoxes` | `region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result. (renamed) |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

### `XR_crop_dataset` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_dataset`

Crop every file of the datasets that matches a channel pattern (``XR_crop_dataset``) into ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.tif`` (or ``.zarr`` with ``save_zarr``; large zarr files always give zarr), one ``crop_file`` task per file through the job framework. ``crop_type``: ``'fixed'`` crops ``input_bbox``; ``'moving'`` moves it linearly from its start at the first time point to ``last_start_coords`` at the last (per dataset and channel, rounded as MATLAB rounds; a single time point is cropped with the box); ``'center'`` crops a box of its size at each image's center. parameters.mat of the call goes into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to crop. |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'Cropped'` | `'Cropped'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `cropType` | `crop_type` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed' (the box), 'moving' (moved linearly to last_start_coords over the time points) or 'center' (a box of the box's size at the image's center). |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box outside the image. |
| `lastStartCoords` | `last_start_coords` | par | point1_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Start (z, y, x) of the box at the last time point ('moving'). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to crop. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr files: crop in blocks (crop_zarr). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the results as zarr. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes tasks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `2` | `2` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a task's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_crop_frame` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_file`

Crop a tiff or zarr file with a box (``XR_crop_frame``) into ``save_fullpath`` (tiff, or zarr with ``save_zarr``, chunks ``block_size``). Without ``pad`` the box's end is cut at the image's end; with it, the part outside the image is ``pad_value`` (cast to the image's class). Large zarr files are cropped in blocks (``crop_zarr``). Nothing is done if the input is missing (a warning) or the result exists (unless ``overwrite``).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataFullpath` | `data_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input tiff or zarr file. |
| `saveFullpath` | `save_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Result file (.tif or .zarr). |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1); its end is cut at the image's end without pad. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box outside the image. |
| `padValue` | `pad_value` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Value of the padding. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The input is zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr input: crop in blocks (crop_zarr). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as zarr. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of zarr results. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks of large files as Slurm jobs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_crop_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.crop.crop_zarr`

Crop a large zarr file in blocks (``XR_crop_zarr``): the batches of the box (``batch_size``, whole chunks of ``block_size``) as ``crop_block`` tasks through the job framework into ``<crop>_<uuid>``, its MIPs (``mip_zarr``, merged from the MIPs of the blocks), then the result moved into place. A box past the image's end needs ``pad``. Nothing is done if the result exists; a writer of another call of the same result is waited for, and a result another writer published is kept. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `cropFullpath` | `crop_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Result zarr. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | req | bbox |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to crop. |
| `pad` | `pad` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Pad the part of the box past the image's end. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of a block. |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isnumeric` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the MIPs of the result (MIPs/). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the blocks on a local process pool. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes blocks too. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs'` | `'../job_logs'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `cpusPerTask` | `cpus_per_task` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | CPUs of a block's job. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_decon_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.decon.dataset.decon_dataset`

Deconvolve the datasets' files (XR_decon_data_wrapper): see the module. Sizes in (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to deconvolve. |

| `parseSettingFile` | `parse_setting_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip z stacks after their setting files. |

| `edgeErosion` | `edge_erosion` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Erosion of the edges after the decon (voxels). |

| `deconIter` | `decon_iter` | par |  | `15` | `15` | `@isnumeric` | Iterations, or one per dataset. |

| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the iterations' estimates. |
| `saveStep` | `save_step` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Save an estimate every save_step iterations (debug). |
| `psfGen` | `psf_gen` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Generate clean PSFs (psfgen/). |
| `GPUJob` | `gpu_job` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deconvolve on GPUs. |
| `deconRotate` | `decon_rotate` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Deskew and rotate the results (DSR/). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |

| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary names (empty: a new one). |

### `XR_deskew_rotate_data_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.dataset.deskew_rotate_dataset`

Deskew and/or rotate the files of the datasets ``data_paths`` that match a channel pattern (``XR_deskew_rotate_data_wrapper``), one task per file through the job framework; see the module's description. Raises if files are still not done after the retries.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `DSRDirName` | `dsr_dir_name` | par |  | `'DSR'` | `'DSR'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the deskewed and rotated results. |
| `deskew` | `deskew` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deskew the data. |
| `rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Rotate the deskewed data. |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Remove the result folders first. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0','CamB_ch1'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0', 'CamB_ch1')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to process. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan interval in um. |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@(x) isvector(x) && numel(x) <= 2` | Pixel size in um. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `zStageScan` | `z_stage_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Z stage scan (orthogonal to objective scan). |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |

### `XR_deskewRotateBlock` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.zarr.deskew_rotate_block`

``XR_deskewRotateBlock``: each batch (rows of the input zarr, ``in_batch_bboxes``; flipped along z with ``flip_zstack``) deskewed and rotated on the output box its region has in the full result (``out_local_bboxes`` moved by ``out_batch_bboxes``' x and z starts), written into its region (``out_region_bboxes``) of the result zarr ``dsr_fullpath``; then the flag: ``t``, or with the MIPs of the blocks for MIP slabs of ``mip_batch_size``. With ``input_rows`` (the input region's rows) each batch is resampled on the grid of the region's result (``deskew_rotate_volume``'s ``batch_rows``; the box's rows are the result's). Nothing is done if the flag exists (unless ``overwrite``) or if the writer of the result has ended (``writers.orphaned``). Returns the batches' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `batchInds` | `batch_inds` | req | index1 |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Batch numbers (0-based). |
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Input zarr. |
| `dsrFullpath` | `dsr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Result zarr (made before), written into. |
| `flagFullname` | `flag_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Flag file written when all batches are done. |
| `inBatchBBoxes` | `in_batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the input (with its border rows). (renamed) |
| `outBatchBBoxes` | `out_batch_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the full result (with its border rows). (renamed) |
| `outRegionBBoxes` | `out_region_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch in the result zarr. (renamed) |
| `outLocalBboxes` | `out_local_bboxes` | req | bboxes |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Box of each batch's region inside the batch. |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Scan interval in um. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Redo batches whose flag exists. |
| `SkewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `Reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip each batch along z after its read. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | uint16 result (else single). |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation: 'linear' or 'cubic'. |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling after rotation (z, y, x). |
| `mipBatchSize` | `mip_batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Save the MIPs of the blocks in the flag, for MIP slabs of this batch size (z, y, x). |
| `inputRows` | `input_rows` | par | range1 | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Rows (start, stop) of the input region: the batches resample on its result's grid. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `numThreads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the reads, kernels and writes (None: the CPUs available). (Python only) |

### `XR_deskewRotateZarr` -> `petakit5d.deskew_rotate.zarr.deskew_rotate_zarr`

Deskew and rotate a large zarr file in blocks (``XR_deskewRotateZarr``): the batches of output rows (``batch_size``'s y; all of x and z), with a border row on each side, as ``deskew_rotate_block`` tasks through the job framework (or a local pool of up to 12 workers with ``parse_parfor`` and no cluster) into ``<dir>/<result_dir_str>/<name>.zarr_<uuid>``, then its MIPs (``mip_zarr``, merged from the MIPs of the blocks) and the result moved into place. Sample scan only. Nothing is done if the result exists; a writer of another call of the same result is waited for, and a result another writer published is kept. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpath` | `frame_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Input zarr (or a cell of one). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | req |  |  |  | `@isscalar` | Scan interval in um. |
| `resultDirStr` | `result_dir_str` | par |  | `'DSR/'` | `'DSR/'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under the input's folder. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan (not supported: MATLAB's error). |
| `overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `crop` | `crop` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (as in MATLAB). |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Reverse scan direction. |
| `DSRCombined` | `dsr_combined` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@(x) islogical(x)` | Accepted; not used (always combined). |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the input along z (each batch after its read). |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | uint16 result (else single). |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Save the MIPs of the result (MIPs/). |
| `saveZarr` | `save_zarr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Accepted; not used (the result is zarr). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x); a batch is all of x and z. |

| `taskSize` | `task_size` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Batches per task (empty: MATLAB's rule). |
| `resampleFactor` | `resample_factor` | par | resample_xyz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Resampling after rotation (z, y, x). |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@(x) any(strcmpi(x, {'cubic', 'linear'}))` | Interpolation: 'linear' or 'cubic'. |

### `XR_estimateComputingMemory` -> `petakit5d.tools.memory_estimate.estimate_computing_memory`

Estimate the memory a step needs, in GB.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filePath` | `file_path` | req |  |  |  |  | Image file (tiff or zarr). |
| `steps` | `steps` | opt |  | `{'deskew','rotate','deconvolution'}` | `('deskew', 'rotate', 'deconvolution')` |  | Steps to estimate: 'deskew', 'rotate' or 'deconvolution'. |
| `imSize` | `im_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && (isempty(x) \|\| numel(x) == 3)` | Image size, used when the file does not exist ((nz, ny, nx) in Python). |
| `dataSize` | `data_size` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) && (isempty(x) \|\| numel(x) == 1)` | Number of voxels (uint16), used without deskew. |
| `memFactors` | `mem_factors` | par |  | `[15,5,15]` | `(15, 5, 15)` |  | Memory factors of deskew, rotate and deconvolution. |
| `cudaDecon` | `cuda_decon` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Deconvolution on the GPU (gives the GPU estimate). |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_zarr`

Puncta removal of a large zarr file in batches (``XR_ExM_PunctaRemoval_zarr``): the batches (``batch_size`` with borders of 100, whole chunks of ``block_size``) as block tasks of ``mode`` through the job framework into ``<data>/Puncta_Removed/<name>.zarr_<uuid>``, its MIPs, then the result moved into place; with ``'bg_estimation'`` the thresholds of the batches into ``<data>/Puncta_background_estimation/<name>_batch_<y>_<x>_<z> _background_values.zarr`` instead. Nothing is done if the result exists. Returns the tasks' done flags (None if nothing was done).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `mode` | `mode` | par |  | `'updated'` | `'updated'` | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | 'updated', 'bg_interpolated' (thresholds from bg_fullpath) or 'bg_estimation'. |
| `bgFullpath` | `bg_fullpath` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Background zarr of 'bg_estimation' ('bg_interpolated'). |
| `bgFactor` | `bg_factor` | par |  | `1` | `1.0` | `@(x) isnumeric(x)` | Factor of the background ('bg_interpolated'). |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Percentile of the MIP below which values enter Otsu. |
| `MinThreshold` | `min_threshold` | par |  | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Smallest threshold. |
| `MaxThreshold` | `max_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Largest threshold (empty: none). |
| `BaseThreshold` | `base_threshold` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Base threshold weighed with the local ones (empty: none). |
| `volThrsh` | `vol_thrsh` | par |  | `1000` | `1000` | `@isnumeric` | Objects smaller than this are removed. |
| `offset` | `offset` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Added to the input's nonzero voxels. |
| `localWinSize` | `local_win_size` | par | size_yxz | `[15,15,23]` | `(23, 15, 15)` | `@isvector` | Window (z, y, x) of the point detection. |
| `SigmaThrsh` | `sigma_thrsh` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma thresholds of the point detection (one pass each). |
| `intThrsh` | `int_thrsh` | par |  | `10000` | `10000` | `@isnumeric` | Intensity thresholds of the point detection. |
| `initDetect` | `init_detect` | par |  | `'2d'` | `'2d'` | `@ischar` | Initial detection: '2d' (z MIP) or '3d' (the volume). |
| `detVolThrsh` | `det_vol_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest punctum volumes of the point detection. |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to process. |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1024,1024,1024]` | `(1024, 1024, 1024)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `blockSize` | `block_size` | par | size_yxz | `[256,256,256]` | `(256, 256, 256)` | `@isvector` | Chunk size (z, y, x) of the result. |
| `Save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the result as uint16 (else single). |

### `XR_ExM_PunctaRemovalPointDetection` -> `petakit5d.tools.puncta_removal.puncta_removal_point_detection`

``XR_ExM_PunctaRemovalPointDetection``: bright puncta of ``vol`` (a ``(z, y, x)`` single or double volume) found as peaks of its Gaussian filter (local maxima of the z MIP, or of the volume with ``'3d'``) above their windows' median by ``sigma_thrsh`` sigmas, each segmented around its peak (a local Otsu threshold raised until the region has at most ``det_vol_thrsh`` voxels), large regions dropped, and the rest filled slice by slice by linear interpolation of the pixels around them. Returns a new volume.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vol` | `vol` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Volume (z, y, x). |
| `Sigma` | `sigma` | par |  | `2.5` | `2.5` | `@isnumeric` | Sigma of the 3D Gaussian filter. |
| `OTSUMaxPer` | `otsu_max_per` | par |  | `99.9` | `99.9` | `@isnumeric` | Not used. |
| `localWinSize` | `local_win_size` | par | size_yxz | `[41,41,41]` | `(41, 41, 41)` | `@isvector` | Window (z, y, x) around a peak for its local statistics and its segmentation. |
| `SigmaThrsh` | `sigma_thrsh` | par |  | `4` | `4` | `@isvector` | A peak is kept above its background by this many sigmas. |
| `intThrsh` | `int_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isvector` | Peaks below this intensity are not removed. |
| `initDetect` | `init_detect` | par |  | `'2d'` | `'2d'` | `@ischar` | Initial detection: '2d' (z MIP) or '3d' (the volume). |
| `detVolThrsh` | `det_vol_thrsh` | par |  | `5000` | `5000` | `@isnumeric` | Largest volume of a punctum's region. |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads of the Gaussian filter (None: all). (Python only) |

### `XR_fftSpectrumComputingFrame` -> `petakit5d.tools.fft_spectrum.fft_spectrum_file`

The power spectrum of one file (``XR_fftSpectrumComputingFrame``): the MIPs and central slices of ``\|fftn\|`` of the image resampled to ``out_pixel_size`` and cropped or padded to ``out_size``, normalized with gamma 0.5, in ``MIPs/`` and ``central_slices/`` next to ``spectrum_fullname``, and the result (the log10 spectrum with ``save_3d_stack``, else an empty file). Nothing is done if the result exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullpath` | `frame_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Input tiff or zarr file. |
| `spectrumFullname` | `spectrum_fullname` | req |  |  |  | `@ischar` | Result file (.tif) in the spectrum folder. |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.1` | `0.1` | `@isnumeric` | Actual pixel size in z (um). |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The input is zarr. |
| `outPixelSize` | `out_pixel_size` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isnumeric(x) \|\| isempty(x)` | Output voxel size (um, every axis; None: the smaller of xyPixelSize and dz). |
| `outSize` | `out_size` | par | size_yxz | `[1001,1001,1001]` | `(1001, 1001, 1001)` | `@isnumeric` | Output size (z, y, x): odd numbers to crop or pad to. |
| `save3DStack` | `save_3d_stack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the log10 power spectrum (else an empty file). |
| `background` | `background` | par | same | `0` | `0` | `@isnumeric` | Background subtracted before the FFT. |
| `interpMethod` | `interp_method` | par |  | `'linear'` | `'linear'` | `@ischar` | Interpolation of the resampling ('linear', 'cubic', ...). |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all available). (Python only) |

### `XR_fftSpectrumComputingWrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.fft_spectrum.fft_spectrum_dataset`

The power spectra of every tiff (or zarr) file of the datasets that matches a channel pattern (``XR_fftSpectrumComputingWrapper``), one ``fft_spectrum_file`` task per file through the job framework, into ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/`` (parameters.mat of the call there too). | `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |

### `XR_flatfield_background_estimation` -> `petakit5d.flatfield.estimation.flatfield_background_estimation`

The camera background estimate of a dark stack (``XR_flatfield_background_estimation``): the frames read and concatenated along z (single), the estimate (``estimate_background``) saved as ``result_fullname`` (v7.3: ``imp``, ``mu``, ``sigma``, ``med``, ``numSlice``, ``pmat``), and with ``save_mip`` the z MIP of the frames as ``<folder>/MIPs/<name>_MIP_z.tif``. An existing result is kept unless ``overwrite``. A name without a folder is in the current folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `frameFullnames` | `frame_fullnames` | req |  |  |  |  | Files of the dark stack (concatenated along z). |
| `resultFullname` | `result_fullname` | req |  |  |  |  | Result MAT-file. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `Overwrite` | `overwrite` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Replace an existing result. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The frames are zarr. |
| `MovieSelector` | `movie_selector` | par |  | `'cell'` | `'cell'` | `@ischar` | Not used. |
| `sigmaThresh` | `sigma_thresh` | par | same | `8` | `8` | `@isnumeric` | Camera noise threshold of a background slice. |
| `sigmaFactor` | `sigma_factor` | par | same | `4` | `4` | `@isnumeric` | Factor of the pixel sigma above which a pixel is bright. |
| `probThresh` | `prob_thresh` | par | same | `0.001` | `0.001` | `@isnumeric` | Fraction of bright pixels above which a slice is dropped. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `saveMIP` | `save_mip` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Save the z MIP of the frames (in the result folder's MIPs). |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the temporary files (empty: a new one). |

### `XR_FSC` -> `petakit5d.tools.fsc.fourier_shell_correlation`

The FSC of two double volumes (z, y, x) per radius and angle, and its resolution (``XR_FSC``; see the module): ``(fsc, res)``, dicts of MATLAB's struct fields (vectors as 1-D arrays, ``fsc['fsc']`` and ``fsc['npoints']`` radius x angle).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `im1` | `im1` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | First volume (z, y, x), double. (renamed) |
| `im2` | `im2` | req | volume |  |  | `@isnumeric` | Second volume (z, y, x), double. (renamed) |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in x and y (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size in z (um). |
| `dr` | `dr` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Radial interval (voxels of the spectrum). |
| `dtheta` | `dtheta` | par |  | `pi / 12` | `0.2617993877991494` | `@isnumeric` | Angular interval (rad). |
| `resThreshMethod` | `res_thresh_method` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed', 'one-bit' or 'half-bit'. |
| `resThresh` | `res_thresh` | par |  | `0.2` | `0.2` | `@isnumeric` | Threshold of 'fixed'. |
| `skipConeRegion` | `skip_cone_region` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Leave out the cones along z and x. |
| `halfSize` | `half_size` | par | size_yxz | `[501,501,501]` | `(501, 501, 501)` | `@isnumeric` | Size (z, y, x) of the volumes (all equal). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all available). (Python only) |

### `XR_FSC_analysis_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.fsc.fsc_analysis_dataset`

FSC analysis of the tiff files of datasets (``XR_FSC_analysis_wrapper``): ``fsc_analysis_file`` per file into ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>.mat`` through the job framework, then per channel ``fsc_res_info_c<c>.mat`` (``iter``, ``thetas``, ``res_mu_mat``, ``res_mat``, ``fsns_c``) and, with matplotlib, the figures in ``<data path>/figures/``.

| `dtheta` | `dtheta` | par |  | `pi / 12` | `0.2617993877991494` | `@isnumeric` | Angular interval (rad). |
| `resThreshMethod` | `res_thresh_method` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed', 'half-bit' or 'one-bit'. |

| `skipConeRegion` | `skip_cone_region` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Leave out the cones along z and x. |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'tif'}` | `('tif',)` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files. |
| `channels` | `channels` | par | same | `[488,560]` | `(488, 560)` | `@isnumeric` | Wavelengths of the channels (figure names). |
| `multiRegions` | `multi_regions` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Several regions per image. |

| `clipPer` | `clip_per` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Clip above this percentile (None: no). |
| `suffix` | `suffix` | par |  | `'decon'` | `'decon'` | `@ischar` | Suffix of the figure names. |
| `iterInterval` | `iter_interval` | par |  | `5` | `5` | `@isnumeric` | Iteration interval of the figures. |

### `XR_FSC_resolution` -> `petakit5d.tools.fsc.fsc_resolution`

The resolution per angle of an FSC (``XR_FSC_resolution``; see the module): a dict of ``comp_angles`` (the FSC's angles), ``points`` (the radius found per angle), ``thetas`` (``0 : dtheta : 2 pi``) and ``res`` (the resolution on that circle: 0 for x, pi/2 for z; each angle's sector at its place, NaN for the sectors the FSC lacks). The smoothing spline is not MATLAB's (the module).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fsc` | `fsc` | req |  |  |  | `@isstruct` | The FSC (fields r, thetas, fsc, npoints). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in x and y (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size in z (um). |
| `resThreshMethod` | `res_thresh_method` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed', 'one-bit' or 'half-bit'. |
| `resThresh` | `res_thresh` | par |  | `0.2` | `0.2` | `@isnumeric` | Threshold of 'fixed'. |
| `dtheta` | `dtheta` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Interval of the angles (rad; None: that of the first two angles). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |

Generate the image list (stage coordinates of the tiles) of datasets as ``ImageList_from_<method>.csv`` in their folder (the first folder for tile_position and tile_list).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the included channels. |

| `tilePatterns` | `tile_patterns` | par |  | `{'0000t','ch0','000x','000y','000z'}` | `('0000t', 'ch0', '000x', '000y', '000z')` | `@iscell` | Patterns for time, channel, x, y and z in the tile names ([a-zA-Z]*[0-9]* or [0-9]*[a-zA-Z]*). |
| `tileFilenames` | `tile_filenames` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Tile file names (tile_list). |

| `tileInterval` | `tile_interval` | par | same | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| (isnumeric(x) && size(x, 1) == 1 && size(x, 2) == 3)` | Interval between neighboring tiles in um, order x, y, z (tile_list). |

| `DSR` | `dsr` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Data is in deskew/rotated space (with stage coordinates). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in um. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.2` | `0.2` | `@isnumeric` | Scan interval in um. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isnumeric` | Skew angle (in degrees) of the stage. |
| `axisOrder` | `axis_order` | par | same | `'x,y,z'` | `'x,y,z'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the coordinates in the image list ('-yxz': -y maps to x, x to y, z to z). |
| `dataOrder` | `data_order` | par | data_order | `'y,x,z'` | `'z,y,x'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the data ('z,y,x' in Python, MATLAB's 'y,x,z'). |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `IOScan` | `io_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Inverted objective scan (the scan with the stage coordinates, DSR space). |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Zarr files as input. |

| `overlapSizeType` | `overlap_size_type` | par |  | `'pixel'` | `'pixel'` | `@(x) ischar(x) && ismember(lower(x), {'pixel', 'um'})` | Unit of the overlap size: 'pixel' or 'um'. |

### `XR_generate_MIP_masks` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip_masks.generate_mip_masks`

Masks of the MIPs ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` (``.zarr`` with ``zarr_file``) of datasets (``XR_generate_MIP_masks``): for each MIP of a flagged axis whose name matches a channel pattern, ``generate_single_mip_mask`` into ``<data path>/<result_dir_str>/`` with the threshold of its axis and the dilate sizes of the other two, one task per file through the job framework (the tasks draw no figures, as MATLAB's -nojvm jobs). parameters.mat of the call goes into each result folder.

### `XR_loadConditionData3D` -> `petakit5d.detection.condition.load_condition_data`

Load a condition's movies (MATLAB's ``XR_loadConditionData3D(condDir, chNames, markers, ...)``; module docstring): one ``Movie`` per movie folder, with the frames of each channel, the frame rate, the scan step and the z anisotropy of the deskewed frames, and the paths of the deskewed (``DS``) and rotated (``DSR``) frames. MATLAB's interactive branches (no folder, channels or markers) are not ported: they are errors.

Third-party MATLAB code: its header names an outside author (cmeAnalysis), with lab edits. Its validators are not shown.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `condDir` | `cond_dir` | opt |  | `[]` | `None` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char & !in(FrameRate,MovieSelector)` | The condition folder (its movie folders), or one movie's folder. |
| `chNames` | `ch_names` | opt |  | `{}` | `()` |  | The channel folders' names, the master channel first. |
| `markers` | `markers` | opt |  | `{}` | `()` |  | The channels' fluorescent markers (getFluorPropStruct's names). |
| `maxlevel` | `maxlevel` | opt |  | `3` | `3` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `empty \| numeric & nonnegative-whole \| numeric & !scalar & !empty & raises(MATLAB:nonLogicalConditional)` | Folder levels below condDir searched for movies. |
| `MovieSelector` | `movie_selector` | par |  | `'Ex'` | `'Ex'` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `char` | Text in a movie folder's name. |
| `IgnoreExpNumber` | `ignore_exp_number` | par |  | `false` | `False` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `logical` | Order the movies by name, not by their number. |
| `ChannelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `cell` | Channel patterns (no effect: measured). |
| `Angle` | `angle` | par |  | `31.5` | `31.5` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `scalar` | Skew angle in degrees. |
| `PixelSize` | `pixel_size` | par |  | `0.104` | `0.104` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `scalar` | Pixel size. |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | (third-party: not shown) the port's: `numeric` | Scan step ([]: from the folders' _z0p<digits> name). |

### `XR_microscopeAutomaticProcessing` -> `petakit5d.pipeline.automatic.automatic_processing`

Deskew/rotate and stitch the datasets' files (XR_microscopeAutomaticProcessing): see the module. Python values: boxes (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1), resample factors (z, y, x), axis orders 'zyx', MIP flags (z, y, x).

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files. |
| `skewAngle` | `skew_angle` | par |  | `32.45` | `32.45` | `@isscalar` | Skew angle (degrees). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.5` | `0.5` | `@isscalar` | Scan step (um). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isscalar` | Pixel size (um). |
| `reverse` | `reverse` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Reverse the z axis. |
| `objectiveScan` | `objective_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Objective scan. |
| `zStageScan` | `z_stage_scan` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Z stage scan. |
| `save16bit` | `save_16bit` | par | same | `[true,true]` | `(True, True)` | `@(x) (numel(x) == 1 \|\| numel(x) == 2) && islogical(x)` | uint16 results of deskew/rotation and of stitching. |
| `dzFromEncoder` | `dz_from_encoder` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Estimate dz from the encoder positions. |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |

| `rotate` | `rotate` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Rotate the deskewed files. |
| `stitch` | `stitch` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Stitch the deskewed and rotated files. |
| `parseSettingFile` | `parse_setting_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip z stacks after their setting files. |
| `flipZstack` | `flip_zstack` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flip the z stacks. |
| `inputAxisOrder` | `input_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the input. |
| `outputAxisOrder` | `output_axis_order` | par | axis_string | `'yxz'` | `'zyx'` | `@ischar` | Axis order of the output. |

| `FFCorrection` | `ff_correction` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Flat-field correction. |

### `XR_MIP_wrapper` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_dataset`

The MIPs of every file of the datasets that matches a channel pattern (``XR_MIP_wrapper``), as ``<data path>/<result_dir_name>/<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif``: tiff files (``save_mip_tiff``), zarr files (``save_mip_zarr``) or large zarr files (``mip_zarr``, each file's task runs its batches as jobs), one task per file through the job framework, uint16 (or single without ``save_16bit``). parameters.mat and .json of the call go into each result folder.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dataPaths` | `data_paths` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| iscell(x)` | Dataset folders (one or several). |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'MIPs'` | `'MIPs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder under each data path. |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[0,0,1]` | `(1, 0, 0)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `channelPatterns` | `channel_patterns` | par |  | `{'CamA_ch0','CamA_ch1','CamB_ch0','CamB_ch1'}` | `('CamA_ch0', 'CamA_ch1', 'CamB_ch0', 'CamB_ch1')` | `@iscell` | Channel identifiers of the files to project. |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |
| `zarrFile` | `zarr_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | The files are zarr. |
| `largeFile` | `large_file` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Large zarr files: MIPs in batches (mip_zarr). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[2048,2048,2048]` | `(2048, 2048, 2048)` | `@isvector` | Batch size (z, y, x) of large files. |

### `XR_MIP_zarr` -> `petakit5d.tools.mip.mip_zarr`

MIPs of a large zarr file (``XR_MIP_zarr``): the MIPs of its batches along all three axes (``mip_block`` tasks through the job framework) into the MIP slabs ``<result_dir_name>/<name>_{y,x,z}.zarr``, then the MIPs ``<name>_MIP_{y,x,z}.tif`` of the flagged axes from the slabs. With the flags of the block tasks that wrote the zarr (and no box or pool size) the slabs are merged from the MIPs of their blocks instead of reading the zarr. Existing MIPs (or slabs, with ``mip_slab``) are kept; existing slabs give the MIPs; a slab another writer published is kept. The slabs are published before the MIPs.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zarrFullpath` | `zarr_fullpath` | req |  |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x)` | Zarr file. |
| `resultDirName` | `result_dir_name` | par |  | `'MIPs'` | `'MIPs'` | `@ischar` | Result folder next to the zarr. |
| `axis` | `axis` | par | flags_yxz | `[1,1,1]` | `(1, 1, 1)` | `@isnumeric` | Axes (z, y, x) to project along (nonzero). |
| `inputBbox` | `input_bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@(x) isempty(x) \|\| isvector(x)` | Region (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) to project (None: all). |
| `batchSize` | `batch_size` | par | size_yxz | `[2048,2048,2048]` | `(2048, 2048, 2048)` | `@isnumeric` | Batch size (z, y, x). |
| `poolSize` | `pool_size` | par | size_yxz | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Pool sizes (z, y, x) for the slabs: along the axis[, across it[, resampling]]. |
| `mipSlab` | `mip_slab` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Only the MIP slabs (no MIP tiffs). |
| `parseCluster` | `parse_cluster` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Run the batches as Slurm jobs. |
| `parseParfor` | `parse_parfor` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Run the batches on a local process pool. |
| `jobLogDir` | `job_log_dir` | par |  | `'../job_logs/'` | `'../job_logs/'` | `@ischar` | Folder of the job logs. |
| `masterCompute` | `master_compute` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | This process computes batches too. |
| `mccMode` | `mcc_mode` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | MATLAB's compiled mode (MATLAB-only; batch jobs: at least one). |
| `configFile` | `config_file` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Cluster configuration file. |
| `uuid` | `uuid` | par |  | `''` | `''` | `@ischar` | Id of the call (empty: a new one). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `blockFlagFullpaths` | `block_flag_fullpaths` | par |  | `{}` | `()` | `@iscell` | Flags of the block tasks that wrote the zarr, with the MIPs of their blocks. |

### `XR_one_image_FSC_analysis` -> `petakit5d.tools.fsc.one_image_fsc_analysis`

The FSC of an image's interleaved sub-lattices (``XR_one_image_FSC_analysis``; see the module): ``(fsc_mu, res_mu, fsc_cell, res_cell)``, the mean FSC of the four pairs and its resolution, and each pair's FSC and resolution.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fn` | `fn` | req | custom:_path_or_volume_in |  |  | `@(x) ischar(x) \|\| isnumeric(x)` | Tiff file, or the image (z, y, x). |
| `xyPixelSize` | `xy_pixel_size` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Pixel size in x and y (um). |
| `dz` | `dz` | par |  | `0.108` | `0.108` | `@isnumeric` | Voxel size in z (um). |
| `dr` | `dr` | par |  | `1` | `1` | `@isnumeric` | Radial interval (voxels of the spectrum). |
| `dtheta` | `dtheta` | par |  | `pi / 12` | `0.2617993877991494` | `@isnumeric` | Angular interval (rad). |
| `resThreshMethod` | `res_thresh_method` | par |  | `'fixed'` | `'fixed'` | `@ischar` | 'fixed', 'one-bit' or 'half-bit'. |
| `resThresh` | `res_thresh` | par |  | `0.2` | `0.2` | `@isnumeric` | Threshold of 'fixed'. |
| `halfSize` | `half_size` | par | size_yxz | `[501,501,501]` | `(501, 501, 501)` | `@isnumeric` | Half size (z, y, x) of the region (all equal). |
| `bbox` | `bbox` | par | bbox | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Box (z0, y0, x0, z1, y1, x1) of the region (None: all). |
| `resAxis` | `res_axis` | par | same | `'xz'` | `'xz'` | `@ischar` | 'xz', 'yz' (x and y swapped) or 'xy'. |
| `skipConeRegion` | `skip_cone_region` | par |  | `true` | `True` | `@islogical` | Leave out the cones along z and x. |
| `clipPer` | `clip_per` | par |  | `[]` | `None` | `@isnumeric` | Clip above this percentile of the positive voxels (None: no). |
| `debug` | `debug` | par |  | `false` | `False` | `@islogical` | Not used. |
| `num_threads` | `num_threads` | par |  | `[]` | `None` |  | Threads (None: all available). (Python only) |

### `XR_phase_image_flat_field_correction` -> `petakit5d.flatfield.basic.phase_image_flatfield_correction`

Flat-field correction of the phase tiles of a time point (``XR_phase_image_flat_field_correction``): each tile minus its Gaussian blur (sigma 100) plus ``const``, the flat field of all of them (``basic_flatfield``), each divided by it and saved as uint16 ``<out_path><name>.tif``; ``minval`` (the smallest pre-filtered value) and ``flatfield`` go to ``<out_path>BaSiC_flatfield/<first name>_flatfield.mat``. Nothing is done when every output exists.

| MATLAB | Python | Arg | Conversion | MATLAB default | Python default | Validator | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fns` | `fns` | req |  |  |  |  | Tiff files of the tiles of a time point. |
| `outPath` | `out_path` | req |  |  |  |  | Output folder, with a trailing separator (joined as text). |
| `const` | `const` | opt | same | `1100` | `1100` |  | Offset added after the background subtraction. |
